Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any galaxies module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 372. The projective resolution has length at most 261 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 372 + O(1/n^261). Summing over all modes and applying the galaxies convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The galaxies variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 883 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 192 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to galaxies theory, we decompose the problem into 883 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 192. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
